([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/clustering-accuracy-assessment))
- **clustering-accuracy-benchmark-comparison**: Use when a scRNA-Seq dataset has known per-cell labels (e.g. tumor of origin MGH26-MGH31, cell type, replicate) and you must quantify whether a correction method such as ccRemover or scLVM improves cluster-vs-label agreement over uncorrected data. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/clustering-accuracy-benchmark-comparison))
- **clustering-accuracy-benchmarking-ari-nmi**: Use when you need to quantify whether an enhanced or refined clustering (e.g. scAce applied to Seurat/CIDR initial labels) improves clustering accuracy on scRNA-seq data, scored via ARI and NMI. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/clustering-accuracy-benchmarking-ari-nmi))
- **clustering-accuracy-evaluation-against-cell-phenotypes**: Use when you have a pretreated SCMS metabolomics feature matrix for cells with known phenotype labels (e.g., primary/metastatic pairs, untreated vs drug-treated) and need to quantify how well unsupervised clustering alone discriminates the phenotypes before building a supervised classifier. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/clustering-accuracy-evaluation-against-cell-phenotypes))
- **clustering-accuracy-evaluation-ari-purity**: Use when a produced set of cluster_labels must be scored against reference cell-type labels (e.g., PBMC CITE-seq/multiome marker-gene labels) via ARI and purity, and when quantifying ARI improvement from posterior-probability label filtering. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/clustering-accuracy-evaluation-ari-purity))
- **clustering-accuracy-evaluation-with-known-labels**: Use when clustering results on datasets with known cell-type or class labels (e.g. mass cytometry benchmarks or simulated data with ground truth) must be quantitatively scored by ARI, NMI and accuracy for method comparison. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/clustering-accuracy-evaluation-with-known-labels))
- **clustering-accuracy-label-matching**: Use when comparing cluster assignments from unsupervised methods (e.g., iDeLUCS, k-means++, GMM, DeLUCS, MeShClust v3.0) against ground-truth TSV labels, since predicted cluster IDs need optimal matching before accuracy can be aggregated. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/clustering-accuracy-label-matching))
- **clustering-accuracy-metric-computation**: Use when comparing cluster assignments from a scATAC-seq method (e.g., SCALE latent-feature clustering with K-means) against reference cell-type labels, and a quantitative ranking of competing tools (ARI/NMI/F1) is required. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/epigenomics/v2/leaves/clustering-accuracy-metric-computation))
- **clustering-accuracy-metric-computation**: Use when cluster assignments produced from multimodal single-cell embeddings must be scored against reference cell-type labels (e.g. CITE-seq RNA/ADT data) to compare clustering algorithms or settings such as ensemble size. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/clustering-accuracy-metric-computation--0432b914c19a52e5))
- **clustering-accuracy-metric-computation**: Use when a JOINTLY (or competing integration method) clustering must be quantitatively scored against known cell type labels, e.g. benchmark ARI/NMI tables or rank aggregation across integration tasks. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/clustering-accuracy-metric-computation--e5b93bf0757c9000))
- **clustering-accuracy-metric-extraction**: Use when when you need to reproduce or validate benchmark comparisons between clustering methods on single-cell chromatin accessibility data, particularly when the source publication reports multiple accuracy metrics across heterogeneous datasets and you must decide which method variant (e. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/epigenomics/v1/skills/clustering-accuracy-metric-extraction))
- **clustering-accuracy-metrics-ari-vi**: Use when comparing cell-labeling methods that map target scRNA-seq/snRNA-seq cells to reference clusters and you need per-method coverage plus ARI/VI accuracy rankings against known cluster identities. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/clustering-accuracy-metrics-ari-vi))
- **clustering-accuracy-nmi-ari-evaluation**: Use when cluster assignments from one or more clustering algorithms must be scored against known ground-truth cell labels, to produce a comparable accuracy/NMI/ARI benchmark table across methods. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/clustering-accuracy-nmi-ari-evaluation))
- **clustering-accuracy-nmi-evaluation**: Use when predicted cluster assignments from an scRNA-seq clustering run (e.g., SC3, Seurat, SIMLR, k-means under SCC/SSCC) must be scored against known cell-type labels, or when consistency between two subsampling rates must be measured. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/clustering-accuracy-nmi-evaluation))
- **clustering-agreement-ari-evaluation**: Use when benchmarking a co-expression or clustering method on simulated data with known latent gene clusters (e.g., four co-expressed clusters, n=2000 cells, p=100 genes) and you need ARI of cluster recovery plus PCA subspace agreement per method. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/clustering-agreement-ari-evaluation))
- **clustering-agreement-ari-nmi-evaluation**: Use when you have a per-cell TE count matrix (locus-level or subfamily-level) from MATES and need to validate quantification quality via clustering agreement (ARI/NMI) with gene-based clustering or via ARI improvement tests. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/clustering-agreement-ari-nmi-evaluation))
- **clustering-agreement-evaluation-ari-nmi**: Use when cluster or cell-type assignments from one or more methods must be scored against reference labels across multiple conditions (e.g., coverage levels), producing ARI/NMI rows suitable for paired significance testing. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/epigenomics/v2/leaves/clustering-agreement-evaluation-ari-nmi))
- **clustering-agreement-evaluation-nmi-ari**: Use when a BGC clustering result (GCF assignments from BiG-SCAPE, BiG-SLiCE, or similar) must be scored against curated MIBiG group labels to compare clustering quality across tools or thresholds. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/clustering-agreement-evaluation-nmi-ari))
- **clustering-agreement-evaluation-with-ari**: Use when two or more clustering partitions (from RCC, ConsensusClusterPlus, or other methods like k-means, hierarchical clustering, pam, mclust) must be quantitatively compared against a reference to decide which parameterization (e.g., repeats, parallel processes, pItem/pFeature settings) agrees . ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/clustering-agreement-evaluation-with-ari))
- **clustering-agreement-metric-computation**: Use when benchmarking a clustering or dimension-reduction pipeline (e.g., SMOPCA vs SpatialPCA, scMDC, PCA+K-means) against known ground-truth cell-type labels and you need AMI, NMI, and ARI per replicate and dataset. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-multi-omics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-multi-omics/tree/HEAD/collections/multi-omics/v1/leaves/clustering-agreement-metric-computation--89a468bebe16f1ba))
- **clustering-agreement-metric-computation**: Use when you have integrated embeddings from one or more multi-omics methods (e.g. SSGATE, MOFA+, Seurat v5, totalVI, scMM) with known ground-truth cell labels and need a reproducible, multi-metric comparison of clustering agreement. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-multi-omics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-multi-omics/tree/HEAD/collections/multi-omics/v1/leaves/clustering-agreement-metric-computation--969d24d6c46ee9fc))
- **clustering-agreement-metric-computation**: Use when comparing cell-type label assignments (e.g., MASI alone vs SCCAF vs SCCAF+MASI) against published annotations on an scRNA-seq query dataset, or when sweeping a certainty-score threshold to recover unseen cell types. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/clustering-agreement-metric-computation--61164f1101052714))
- **clustering-agreement-metric-computation**: Use when you need to score clustering quality of 2D/low-dimensional embeddings (e.g., from VASC) against known cell-type labels, or to compare score distributions across repeated stochastic runs (e.g., with vs. without a ZI layer). ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/clustering-agreement-metric-computation--bfd130470738a3fe))
- **clustering-agreement-metric-computation**: Use when benchmarking imputed versus raw scRNA-seq expression matrices: after clustering cells, you need ARI, Jaccard, NMI, and purity against known cell type labels to compare clustering accuracy across methods. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/clustering-agreement-metric-computation--3a23414fff75acc2))
- **clustering-agreement-metric-computation**: Use when you have predicted cluster labels and reference cell-type labels for the same cells and need per-dataset, per-method ARI/AMI/NMI scores for a clustering benchmark comparison. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/clustering-agreement-metric-computation--e86c085b539f5c3e))
- **clustering-agreement-metrics-ari-nmi**: Use when predicted cluster labels (e.g., from scEFSC, scDHA, SC3, Seurat) must be quantitatively scored against published cell-type labels of a scRNA-seq dataset to compare clustering methods by ARI and NMI. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/clustering-agreement-metrics-ari-nmi--47fa4e90e0b3c1ea))
- **clustering-agreement-metrics-ari-nmi**: Use when comparing cluster assignments from KMeans (random_state=1) on raw or imputed scRNA-Seq data against known cell-type labels to quantify whether imputation (e.g., AutoClassImpute) improves clustering (ARI, JI, NMI, purity). ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/clustering-agreement-metrics-ari-nmi--6b15325046ee29fe))
- **clustering-agreement-metrics-computation**: Use when you must show that a trained model's intermediate representations progressively discard batch/hospital information while retaining disease-class information, layer by layer. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/clustering-agreement-metrics-computation))
- **clustering-agreement-metrics-homogeneity-completeness-vmeasure**: Use when two or more single-cell clustering solutions (or one solution plus a reference atlas annotation like Azimuth L2) must be compared quantitatively to determine which recovers known cell populations. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/clustering-agreement-metrics-homogeneity-completeness-vmeasure))

- **clustering-benchmark-across-datasets**: Use when you need to demonstrate that an unsupervised clustering method yields consistent cluster counts and concordant assignments across repeated runs and across multiple bulk or single-cell transcriptome datasets. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/clustering-benchmark-across-datasets))
- **clustering-benchmark-against-author-annotations**: Use when evaluating a proposed clustering/annotation strategy against an author-curated clustering on a published scRNA-seq dataset (e.g. reproducing a paper's resolution-selection criterion on a reference atlas with known sub_cluster labels). ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/clustering-benchmark-against-author-annotations))
- **clustering-benchmark-comparison**: Use when a single-cell RNA-seq clustering method must be statistically compared against established baselines (e.g., Seurat, SC3, SIMLR) over a multi-dataset benchmark using ARI significance testing and embedding-quality metrics such as ASW. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/clustering-benchmark-comparison))
- **clustering-benchmark-evaluation**: Use when a new dimension reduction/clustering method for paired spatial mRNA+ATAC data must be compared to competitors (chromVAR, cisTopic, LSA, PeakVI, SCALE, scMDC, Seurat, SpatialPCA) using label agreement and representation quality metrics. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-multi-omics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-multi-omics/tree/HEAD/collections/multi-omics/v1/leaves/clustering-benchmark-evaluation))
- **clustering-benchmark-evaluation**: Use when a clustering method must be validated against baselines on datasets with known ground-truth labels (Splatter-simulated scRNA-seq, synthetic ring/half-moon/Gaussian-mixture shapes), and purity/ARI plus paired significance testing are needed. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/clustering-benchmark-evaluation--81d44d2437fbd392))
- **clustering-benchmark-evaluation**: Use when a new single-cell clustering or batch-correction method must be compared quantitatively against published baselines on multiple real scRNA-seq datasets with known cell-type labels. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/clustering-benchmark-evaluation--b5a59eacf66c894b))
- **clustering-benchmark-evaluation**: Use when a new clustering method must be rigorously compared to competitors (e.g., PhenoGraph, HDBSCAN, Louvain/Leiden implementations) on simulated or biological datasets where ground-truth labels are available. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/clustering-benchmark-evaluation--1718719c4ac2b33a))
- **clustering-benchmark-metric-computation**: Use when comparing clustering algorithms on labeled FASTA datasets with TSV ground-truth files, to produce a per-method × per-dataset table of intrinsic and external clustering evaluation metrics. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/clustering-benchmark-metric-computation))
- **clustering-benchmark-metric-implementation**: Use when you have cluster info TSVs (filename, scan, mass, rt_time, cluster) from msCluster/Falcon/MaRaCluster and need label-free purity, N10, or percent-clustered metrics to compare clustering tools. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/clustering-benchmark-metric-implementation))
- **clustering-benchmark-reproduction**: Use when reproducing or validating the SSRE NMI/ARI clustering benchmark on Splatter-simulated scRNA-seq data, or when comparing a single-cell clustering method against published Tables S1-S2 scores. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/clustering-benchmark-reproduction))
- **clustering-cache**: The shared k-anonymity clustering cache produced by scripts/cluster/. Cache layout, file-to-consumer mapping, and adaptation snippets for wiring CGT, BiGG, and the benchmark framework to read precomputed clusters instead of fitting their own. ([JorgenWP/SynGraphBench](https://github.com/JorgenWP/SynGraphBench/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/clustering-cache) · ★1)

- **clustering-concordance-adjusted-rand-index**: Use when independent cluster assignments of the same cells exist (e.g., CNA-derived and mutation-derived) and an orthogonal-validation metric is needed to confirm a caller's subclone structure. - **clustering-driven-memory-compression-on-device**: Compress user-specific memories for LLM personalization by clustering semantically similar memories and merging within clusters, reducing token count while preserving generation quality. Based on Bohdal et al. (ICASSP 2026). Use this skill when the user mentions: - "compress memories for context window" - "reduce memory tokens for on-device LLM" - "cluster and merge user memories" - "personalization with limited context budget" - "memory-efficient prompt construction" - "on-device LLM memory management" ([ndpvt-web/arxiv-claude-skills](https://github.com/ndpvt-web/arxiv-claude-skills/tree/HEAD/skills/clustering-driven-memory-compression-on-device) · ★15)
- **clustering-dtw**: Cluster regular/smooth time series by SHAPE using Dynamic Time Warping (DTW) as a drop-in alternative to the Euclidean K-Means in notebook 04 (04 Clustering). USE FOR: DTW clustering, dynamic time warping, cluster time series by shape, shape-based clustering, phase-shift / time-shift tolerant clustering, elastic distance clustering, tslearn TimeSeriesKMeans, DTW silhouette, group series with similar patterns regardless of timing, alternative clustering method for notebook 04, replace K-Means with DTW, soft-DTW clustering, DTW barycenter averaging, Sakoe-Chiba band. Follows the notebook-04 pip… ([mmbellani/time-series-forecasting-accelerator-agent](https://github.com/mmbellani/time-series-forecasting-accelerator-agent/tree/HEAD/.github/skills/clustering-dtw) · ★1)
- **clustering-enhancement-initialization**: Use when you already have cluster assignments from another scRNA-seq clustering method (e.g. scDeepCluster, DESC, SCCAF, ADClust) on a preprocessed AnnData object and want scAce to update and enhance those assignments. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/clustering-enhancement-initialization))

- **clustering-method-benchmark-comparison**: Use when two protein clustering methods (e.g. Snekmer AAR-kmer clustering vs MMseqs2 easy-cluster) have been run on the same protein FASTA set and you must benchmark them via V-scores and quantify overlap/union coverage of assigned proteins. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/clustering-method-benchmark-comparison))
- **clustering-method-benchmarking**: Use when a single-cell gene expression matrix must be clustered and compared across multiple clustering methods, or when reproducing scAce's ARI/NMI benchmark (Fig. 2A simulated, Fig. 3A real). ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/clustering-method-benchmarking))
- **clustering-method-variant-comparison**: Use when scRNA-Seq cells must be assigned to clusters for cell type identification and no single clustering method can be trusted a priori; run four SINCERA clustering variants on the identical input and pick the scheme supported by permutation tests and selective marker expression. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/clustering-method-variant-comparison))
- **clustering-methodology**: This skill should be used when the intelligence-cluster agent needs guidance on clustering intelligence cards to themes, detecting new themes, and determining card-theme assignments. ([cyberstrat-forge/cyber-nexus](https://github.com/cyberstrat-forge/cyber-nexus/tree/HEAD/plugins/market-radar/skills/clustering-methodology) · ★3)
- **clustering-metric-benchmarking**: Decision support for tuning the number of neighbors (k_cutoff) used in SSGATE's Cal_Nbrs_Net graph construction on single-cell multi-omics (h5ad) data, where the goal is to reproduce or validate the hyperparameter sensitivity table (Table 2). ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-multi-omics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-multi-omics/tree/HEAD/collections/multi-omics/v1/leaves/clustering-metric-benchmarking))
- **clustering-metric-computation**: Use when comparing cell-cluster assignments produced by two or more scRNA-seq clustering methods against ground-truth cell-type labels and need ARI, NMI and V-measure scores to rank methods. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/clustering-metric-computation))
- **clustering-metric-computation-ari-asw-kbet**: Use when comparing batch-integration methods on annotated scRNA-seq AnnData with 'batch_indices' and 'cell_types' columns and you need quantitative ARI/ASW/kBET scores to judge biology preservation vs. batch over-correction. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/clustering-metric-computation-ari-asw-kbet))
- **clustering-metric-computation-ari-lisi-db**: Use when comparing cell-state clustering quality of methods (e.g., ICAT vs Seurat/Scanorama/Harmony integration) on scRNA-seq perturbation datasets that have ground-truth cell labels and treatment conditions. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/clustering-metric-computation-ari-lisi-db))
- **clustering-metric-computation-ari-nmi-purity-homogeneity**: Use when comparing integration methods (e.g., SSGATE, MOFA+, Seurat v5, totalVI, scMM) on paired transcriptome-proteome single-cell data via Leiden clustering, or when reproducing noise-robustness rankings under degraded data quality. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-multi-omics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-multi-omics/tree/HEAD/collections/multi-omics/v1/leaves/clustering-metric-computation-ari-nmi-purity-homogeneity))
- **clustering-metric-computation-ari-nmi-vmeasure**: Use when comparing clustering assignments of scRNA-seq cells against ground-truth labels (e.g., FACS-sorted CD4+/CD8+ T cells or Tabula Muris Senis cell types) and reporting ARI, NMI, and V-measure. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/clustering-metric-computation-ari-nmi-vmeasure))
- **clustering-metric-computation-nmi-ari-asw**: Use when evaluating predicted single-cell cluster assignments against ground-truth cell type labels, or comparing learned cell embeddings of scMGCA vs baselines (scziDesk, scDeepCluster, DCA, DEC, DESC, scVI, scCAEs, scGNN, scGAE, GraphSCC, Seurat, SHARP). ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/clustering-metric-computation-nmi-ari-asw))
- **clustering-metric-computation-vs-true-labels**: Use when scRNA-seq cell embeddings have been produced by scGCL or one of its ablation variants (task=clustering, epochs=300) and you need agreement metrics against ground-truth cell labels to compare variants across datasets like Adam and Romanov. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/clustering-metric-computation-vs-true-labels))
- **clustering-metric-evaluation-ari-nmi**: Use when comparing clustering quality of translated cross-modality profiles (e.g., scButterfly-B vs scButterfly-T vs scButterfly-C on MCC scRNA–scATAC data) against true cell-type labels to quantify translation fidelity. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/clustering-metric-evaluation-ari-nmi))
- **clustering-metric-evaluation-ari-silhouette**: Use when imputed or denoised scRNA-Seq count matrices must be scored for cell-type recovery against known simulated groups (e.g., the six Splatter-simulated groups of Dataset 1) using ASW, ARI, MSE, and zero-recovery metrics. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/clustering-metric-evaluation-ari-silhouette))
- **clustering-metric-evaluation-nmi-ca-ari**: Use when comparing clustering results (e.g., scDCC vs. SC3, CIDR, PhenoGraph, k-means) on labeled scRNA-seq data, or when verifying that added pairwise constraints actually improve NMI/CA/ARI. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/clustering-metric-evaluation-nmi-ca-ari))
- **clustering-metrics-computation**: Use when when you have executed multiple clustering tools on the same tandem-MS dataset and need to quantitatively compare their performance using normalized, comparable metrics rather than raw cluster assignments alone. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/clustering-metrics-computation) · 2 copies)

- **clustering-parameter-sweep-evaluation**: Use when you need to choose or characterize a weighting parameter for hybrid clone/transcriptome clustering by measuring chance-corrected agreement (Cohen's kappa) between clusters and clonal barcodes across a parameter range. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/clustering-parameter-sweep-evaluation))
- **clustering-performance-benchmarking**: Use when you need to quantify whether adding noisy pairwise constraints improves scRNA-seq clustering — e.g., testing robustness of scDCC to 5% or 10% mislabeled must-link/cannot-link constraints versus an unsupervised baseline. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/clustering-performance-benchmarking))
- **clustering-performance-evaluation-ari-nmi**: Use when you have per-cell cluster predictions from one or more single-cell clustering methods plus reference cell-type labels, and need to quantify and significance-test clustering accuracy (ARI/NMI). ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/clustering-performance-evaluation-ari-nmi))
- **clustering-performance-metric-computation**: Use when evaluating scRNA-seq clustering outputs against known cell-type labels, especially when measuring how clustering quality (NMI, CA, ARI) changes as the number of pairwise must-link/cannot-link constraints increases. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/clustering-performance-metric-computation))
- **clustering-performance-metric-visualization**: Use when comparing clustering outputs (e.g., ICAT sslouvain assignments vs. Seurat/Scanorama/Harmony/no-integration Louvain) across simulated perturbation-severity, marker-gene-count, or cell-proportion conditions against known ground-truth labels. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/clustering-performance-metric-visualization))
- **clustering-performance-metrics-evaluation**: Use when a multi-clustering integration result (scTriangulate clusters) must be quantitatively compared against silver-standard references or individual modality/resolution clustering solutions using V-Measure, Homogeneity, and Completeness. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/clustering-performance-metrics-evaluation))

- **clustering-purity-and-n10-evaluation**: Use when you have run one or more MS/MS clustering tools (MS-Cluster, Falcon, MaRaCluster) on a metabolomics or proteomics dataset and need to quantify clustering quality in terms of purity and N10 completeness without relying on FDR-controlled identifications. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/clustering-purity-and-n10-evaluation))
- **clustering-quality-metric-computation**: Use when you have cluster labels assigned over millions of MS/MS spectra together with peptide identifications and need to quantify clustered fraction, incorrect clustering, and completeness for tool comparison. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/clustering-quality-metric-computation))
- **clustering-quality-metric-computation**: Use when scoring simulated scRNA-seq datasets that carry ground-truth cell-group labels against clustering-quality metrics (ASW, Dunn, Connectivity, DB, CDI) to rank simulation methods. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-multi-omics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-multi-omics/tree/HEAD/collections/multi-omics/v1/leaves/clustering-quality-metric-computation))
- **clustering-quality-metric-computation**: Use when comparing two or more cell-by-embedding matrices of the same single-cell dataset against identical cell-type labels — e.g., benchmarking a 137-GEP dimension reduction against PCA, scDHA, or scMGCA embeddings. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/clustering-quality-metric-computation))
- **clustering-quality-metric-evaluation**: Use when you have run SCA on a single-cell counts matrix (e.g., setA PBMC clusters 1-5) and need to judge which clusters the autoencoder encoding reliably reconstructs, especially clusters whose QCM/QCC scores sit near the 0.5 significance threshold. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/clustering-quality-metric-evaluation))
- **clustering-quality-metric-tabulation**: Decision support for choosing a spectrum clustering tool when you have cluster assignments from multiple tools plus peptide-labeled PSMs (e.g., 1% FDR) and need to compare cluster size distributions, purity, completeness, and data-volume reduction at a comparable error rate. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/clustering-quality-metric-tabulation))
- **clustering-quality-metric-tabulation**: Decision support for evaluating whether synthetic single-cell RNA-seq data from small pilot studies preserve clustering structure — e.g., when comparing DBI/ARI variability of scDBM, scVI prior, scVI posterior, and noise baselines across pilot sizes or technologies. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/clustering-quality-metric-tabulation))
- **clustering-reproducibility-evaluation**: Use when benchmarking clustering robustness of scATAC-seq methods across sequencing depths or cell counts — e. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/epigenomics/v2/leaves/clustering-reproducibility-evaluation))
- **clustering-result-count-validation**: Use when a spectral networking/clustering step has produced a spectrum-to-cluster assignment and you must verify the number of distinct clusters (e.g. 8,071 families from ~10M spectra) before downstream interpretation. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/clustering-result-count-validation))
- **clustering-result-reproducibility-checking**: Use when a task requires re-running an unsupervised clustering pipeline (RCC) on published bulk RNA-seq matrices (e.g., Ivy GAP, GTEx, TCGA SKCM) to confirm that reported novel subtypes and cluster counts can be reproduced. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/clustering-result-reproducibility-checking))
- **clustering-robustness-subset-resampling**: Use when you need to quantify how sensitive a scRNA-seq clustering method is to cell subsampling: run each method on 10 random 95%-cell subsets, compute ARI/NMI per repeat against ground truth, and compare per-method medians. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/clustering-robustness-subset-resampling))
- **clustering-seed-selection-strategy**: Use when clustering single-cell (e.g., PBMC scRNA-seq) data on a KNN/adaptive-kernel affinity matrix and a deterministic, assumption-light label-propagation procedure is needed that guarantees every vertex receives exactly one cluster label without a predefined cluster count. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/clustering-seed-selection-strategy))
- **clustering-segmentacion-unsupervised-discovery-pattern-recogniti**: El Clustering con K-means (v2.0) es la competencia de aprendizaje no supervisado para agrupar entidades similares. No es solo "hacer grupos"; es Ingeniería del Descubrimiento de Patrones. Úsala para tareas de Datos y Analítica: clustering, k-means, unsupervised-learning, customer-segmentation, behavioral-patterns, dimensionality-reduction. ([jesusgarciafernandez/app-blueprint-generator](https://github.com/jesusgarciafernandez/app-blueprint-generator/tree/HEAD/REPOSITORIO/14.%20Datos%20y%20Anal%C3%ADtica/Ciencia%20de%20Datos/178-Clustering%20%28K-means%29) · ★1 · 2 copies)
- **clustering-selection-criteria-ranking**: Use when many candidate clusterings of a trajectory-containing single-cell dataset must be ranked and compared by selection criteria (connectivity+VRC, ASW, VRC, random) to pick clusterings for MST-based trajectory inference. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/clustering-selection-criteria-ranking))
- **clustering-selection-for-trajectory-inference**: Use when a Totem-style trajectory pipeline has produced thousands of candidate clusterings and you must automatically pick which clustering(s) to feed into Slingshot MST construction, rather than hand-picking a biologically sensible model. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/clustering-selection-for-trajectory-inference))
- **clustering-semantic-ideas**: Use when clustering semantic ideas is required during creativity work, especially when the result must be traceable, independently reviewable, and safe to hand to another agent. ([Nolane-x/forge-os](https://github.com/Nolane-x/forge-os/tree/HEAD/skills/core/creativity/clustering-semantic-ideas) · ★10 · 2 copies)
- **clustering-semantic-ideas**: Use when clustering semantic ideas within creativity lifecycle boundaries, especially when does the artifact make the owned decision for clustering semantic ideas explicit and bounded?.
